- **gcms-chromatogram-baseline-removal**: Use when raw GC/MS chromatogram files (e.g. CDF/NetCDF) contain baseline drift and noise that must be removed before compound deconvolution, alignment, or metabolite identification in an untargeted metabolomics pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-chromatogram-noise-filtering))
- **gcms-component-area-aggregation**: Use when you have an Agilent Unknowns Analysis (or similar) GC-MS library-hit table and need to pull aggregated component areas for a defined chemical list across all samples in a batch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-component-area-aggregation))
- **gcms-data-processing**: Use when raw GC–MS (or LC–MS) chromatogram data must be converted into marker-feature identifications for metabolomics/chemometrics, and conventional peak picking (e.g. XCMS) is producing false peak detections or unreliable signal acquisition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-data-processing))
- **gcms-deconvolution**: Deconvolute gas chromatography mass spectra into component peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/router_collection/leaves/gcms-deconvolution) · ★16)
- **gcms-deconvolution-feature-scoring**: Use when a MSHub/GNPS GC-MS deconvolution feature/quantification table must be scored for deconvolution confidence and filtered to discard chimeric or irreproducible compound fragmentation patterns before annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-deconvolution-feature-scoring))
- **gcms-deconvolution-identification-workflow**: Use when you have GC-MS data (mzML / CDF, typically EI) and want deconvolved, retention-index-validated compound identifications — spectral deconvolution of co-eluting peaks, EI library matching, RI calibration, and differential analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/workflows/gcms-deconvolution-and-identification) · 2 copies)
- **gcms-ei-spectral-edge-analysis**: Use when you have EI molecular networking output (edges table and annotated spectra/consensus spectra) from deconvoluted GC-MS data and need to test whether cosine-similarity edges reflect structural similarity based on chemical class, despite the molecular ion often being absent from EI spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-ei-spectral-edge-analysis))
- **gcms-example-dataset-processing**: Use when the input is raw two-dimensional chromatography–MS data (GC–MS or LC–MS, m/z vs retention time) and the goal is untargeted marker-feature identification while avoiding peak-picking false detections. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-example-dataset-processing))
- **gcms-hit-table-filtering**: Use when the input is a raw aggregated GC-MS library-hit CSV from peak detection software (e.g. Agilent Unknowns Analysis) and you must decide which tentative compound identifications to keep before aggregating peak areas across samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-hit-table-filtering))
- **gcms-internal-standard-dose-gradient-analysis**: Use when a multiclass metabolomic peak table contains internal standards spiked at known levels (e.g. 6/12/25/50 µL classes) and you need to select or validate a NOREVA processing workflow by whether it preserves the true dose gradient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-internal-standard-dose-gradient-analysis))
- **gcms-library-hit-table-parsing**: Use when a GC-MS vendor output (e.g. Agilent Unknowns Analysis hit table) must be converted into a uafR-compatible data frame with exact column names for downstream spreadOut()/mzExacto() processing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-library-hit-table-parsing))
- **gcms-metabolomics-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when starting from a raw GC/MS metabolomics intensity matrix (e.g., 3226 features x 57 samples) whose columns are sample groups, and a variance-stabilized, centered feature table is needed before metabolite identification or biomarker discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-metabolomics-matrix-preprocessing))
- **gcms-molecular-networking**: Use when clustering co-eluted EI GC-MS compounds into similarity networks: deconvoluted spectra exist but molecular ions/precursor masses are absent, so edges must come from cosine similarity of fragmentation spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-molecular-networking))
- **gcms-peak-detection-parameter-optimization**: Use when preprocessing GC-MS metabolomics data (mzML/mzData/NetCDF) with WiPP and needing to select centWave or matchedFilter peak-detection parameters objectively instead of by hand-tuning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-peak-detection-parameter-optimization))
- **gcms-preprocessing-results-analysis**: Use when you have per-algorithm classified peak-detection tables from a WiPP run and need to measure whether the merged/integrated high-quality peak set exceeds each individual algorithm's high-quality yield. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-preprocessing-results-analysis))
- **gcms-preprocessing-workflow-configuration**: Use when preprocessing large-scale GC-MS metabolomics datasets (mzML/mzData/NetCDF) where peak detection parameters for embedded algorithms (centWave, matchedFilter) must be optimised without manual tuning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-preprocessing-workflow-configuration))
- **gcms-quantification-table-generation**: Use when a GC/MS untargeted metabolomics analysis needs to be finalized into a compound-by-sample quantification table after deconvolution, alignment, and identification, and the workflow must integrate names, match factors, and areas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-quantification-table-generation))
- **gcms-query-spectrum-preparation**: Use when measured EI-MS query spectra (e.g., compounds absent from the NIST 2017 library) must be loaded into FastEI and identified by embedding-based nearest-neighbor search against IN_SILICO_LIBRARY.db. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-query-spectrum-preparation))
- **gcms-spectral-deconvolution**: Use when raw GC/MS chromatograms (netCDF or mzXML) contain co-eluting compounds with extensive EI fragmentation and you need pure compound spectra and integrated areas per sample rather than fragment-ion peak picking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-spectral-deconvolution))
- **gcms-spectral-library-matching**: Use when deconvoluted GC-MS spectra are available in a .mgf file (e.g. from GNPS-MSHub auto-deconvolution) and compound identifications are needed via reference-library spectral matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-spectral-library-matching))
